1	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
2	import { AnimatePresence, motion, useReducedMotion } from "framer-motion";
3	import { Mic, Pause, Play } from "lucide-react";
4	
5	/**
6	 * The method, performed.
7	 *
8	 * A looping demonstration of one stimulus worked the way the drill asks: each
9	 * piece read, told back out loud, coloured, and then the whole argument
10	 * recalled with the text gone. It plays on the door because the one thing that
11	 * makes an unfamiliar procedure click is watching somebody do it once, and
12	 * because the procedure's strangest moment -- the stimulus vanishing -- is
13	 * better seen than described.
14	 *
15	 * Everything here is synthetic and says so. The stimulus is written for the
16	 * demo, the transcript is authored, the clock is a prop. Nothing on it is a
17	 * measurement of anyone.
18	 */
19	
20	const STIMULUS = [
21	  "City planners argue that widening Elm Street will ease congestion.",
22	  "But in the three cities that widened comparable roads, traffic rose by roughly a fifth within two years.",
23	  "So widening Elm Street will probably make congestion worse, not better.",
24	];
25	
26	const TELL = [
27	  "So the planners want to widen Elm Street because they think it will cut the traffic.",
28	  "But three other cities did the same thing and traffic actually went up, like twenty percent, over two years.",
29	  "So the author is saying widening it will probably make things worse.",
30	];
31	
32	const COLOUR = ["Classic. Every city says that.", "Twenty percent is a lot. Who counted?", "Probably. Not definitely."];
33	
34	const RECALL =
35	  "Planners say widen Elm Street to ease congestion. Three cities widened similar roads and traffic went up about a fifth. So it will probably make it worse.";
36	
37	const DETAILS: { label: string; carried: boolean }[] = [
38	  { label: "Elm Street", carried: true },
39	  { label: "planners", carried: true },
40	  { label: "ease congestion", carried: true },
41	  { label: "three cities", carried: true },
42	  { label: "up a fifth", carried: true },
43	  { label: "within two years", carried: false },
44	  { label: "worse, not better", carried: true },
45	];
46	
47	type Kind = "read" | "tell" | "colour" | "space" | "recall" | "tally" | "hold";
48	interface Beat {
49	  kind: Kind;
50	  i: number;
51	  ms: number;
52	}
53	
54	const BEATS: Beat[] = [
55	  ...[0, 1, 2].flatMap((i): Beat[] => [
56	    { kind: "read", i, ms: 1900 },
57	    { kind: "tell", i, ms: 2600 },
58	    { kind: "colour", i, ms: 1500 },
59	  ]),
60	  { kind: "space", i: 3, ms: 900 },
61	  { kind: "recall", i: 3, ms: 4200 },
62	  { kind: "tally", i: 3, ms: 1900 },
63	  { kind: "hold", i: 3, ms: 2600 },
64	];
65	
66	const STEP_LABEL: Record<Kind, string> = {
67	  read: "01 · Read it",
68	  tell: "02 · Tell it back",
69	  colour: "03 · Colour it",
70	  space: "Space",
71	  recall: "04 · Recall it",
72	  tally: "Carried",
73	  hold: "Carried",
74	};
75	
76	/**
77	 * Reveals `text` a character at a time across `ms`, starting over each time
78	 * `on` flips true. When `on` goes false the text stays where it got to, so a
79	 * retelling remains readable while the colour lands beside it.
80	 */
81	function useTypewriter(text: string, on: boolean, ms: number, instant: boolean): string {
82	  const [n, setN] = useState(instant ? text.length : 0);
83	  useEffect(() => {
84	    if (instant) {
85	      setN(text.length);
86	      return;
87	    }
88	    if (!on) return;
89	    let raf = 0;
90	    const t0 = performance.now();
91	    setN(0);
92	    const tick = (t: number) => {
93	      const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / ms);
94	      setN(Math.floor(k * text.length));
95	      if (k < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
96	    };
97	    raf = requestAnimationFrame(tick);
98	    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
99	  }, [text, on, ms, instant]);
100	  return text.slice(0, n);
101	}
102	
103	function Waveform({ live }: { live: boolean }) {
104	  return (
105	    <span className="rd-wave" data-live={live || undefined} aria-hidden="true">
106	      {Array.from({ length: 7 }, (_, i) => (
107	        <i key={i} style={{ animationDelay: `${(i * 0.09).toFixed(2)}s` }} />
108	      ))}
109	    </span>
110	  );
111	}
112	
113	function clock(seconds: number): string {
114	  const m = Math.floor(seconds / 60);
115	  const s = seconds % 60;
116	  return `${m}:${String(s).padStart(2, "0")}`;
117	}
118	
119	export function RecallDemo() {
120	  const reduced = useReducedMotion();
121	  const [idx, setIdx] = useState(0);
122	  const [playing, setPlaying] = useState(true);
123	  const [seconds, setSeconds] = useState(0);
124	  const timer = useRef<number | null>(null);
125	
126	  const running = playing && !reduced;
127	
128	  useEffect(() => {
129	    if (!running) return;
130	    timer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setIdx((i) => (i + 1) % BEATS.length), BEATS[idx].ms);
131	    return () => {
132	      if (timer.current) window.clearTimeout(timer.current);
133	    };
134	  }, [idx, running]);
135	
136	  useEffect(() => {
137	    if (!running) return;
138	    const id = window.setInterval(() => setSeconds((s) => (s + 1) % 1500), 1000);
139	    return () => window.clearInterval(id);
140	  }, [running]);
141	
142	  // Reduced motion: the finished state, held, with nothing moving.
143	  const beat = reduced ? BEATS[BEATS.length - 1] : BEATS[idx];
144	  const phase = beat.kind;
145	  const sentence = beat.i;
146	  const gone = phase === "recall" || phase === "tally" || phase === "hold";
147	  const spacePressed = phase === "space";
148	
149	  const tellText = useTypewriter(TELL[Math.min(sentence, 2)], phase === "tell", 2000, Boolean(reduced));
150	  const recallText = useTypewriter(RECALL, phase === "recall", 3600, Boolean(reduced));
151	  const showTally = phase === "tally" || phase === "hold";
152	  const carried = DETAILS.filter((d) => d.carried).length;
153	
154	  return (
155	    <div className="rd-demo" data-phase={phase}>
156	      <div className="rd-demo-bar">
157	        <span className="rd-demo-dots" aria-hidden="true">
158	          <i />
159	          <i />
160	          <i />
161	        </span>
162	        <span className="rd-demo-title">Read, Recall, Colour</span>
163	        <span className="rd-demo-step" aria-live="polite">
164	          {STEP_LABEL[phase]}
165	        </span>
166	        <span className="rd-demo-rec">
167	          <i className="rd-rec-dot" data-live={running || undefined} aria-hidden="true" />
168	          <span className="rd-demo-clock">{clock(seconds)}</span>
169	        </span>
170	        <button
171	          type="button"
172	          className="rd-demo-play"
173	          onClick={() => setPlaying((p) => !p)}
174	          aria-label={playing ? "Pause the demonstration" : "Play the demonstration"}
175	          disabled={Boolean(reduced)}
176	        >
177	          {playing && !reduced ? <Pause size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} /> : <Play size={13} strokeWidth={2.4} />}
178	        </button>
179	      </div>
180	
181	      <div className="rd-demo-body">
182	        <div className="rd-paper" data-gone={gone || undefined}>
183	          <span className="rd-demo-label">Stimulus</span>
184	          <p className="rd-stim">
185	            {STIMULUS.map((s, i) => (
186	              <span
187	                key={i}
188	                className="rd-stim-line"
189	                data-active={!gone && i === sentence ? "true" : undefined}
190	                data-done={!gone && i < sentence ? "true" : undefined}
191	              >
192	                {s}{" "}
193	              </span>
194	            ))}
195	          </p>
196	          <AnimatePresence>
197	            {gone && (
198	              <motion.p
199	                className="rd-gone"
200	                initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
201	                animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
202	                exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
203	                transition={{ duration: 0.5, delay: 0.5, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
204	              >
205	                The stimulus is gone. Say the whole argument back from memory.
206	              </motion.p>
207	            )}
208	          </AnimatePresence>
209	          <div className="rd-keyrow" aria-hidden="true">
210	            <span className="rd-key" data-pressed={spacePressed || undefined}>
211	              space
212	            </span>
213	            <span className="rd-keyhint">{gone ? "once more to move on" : "when you finish the piece"}</span>
214	          </div>
215	        </div>
216	
217	        <div className="rd-voice">
218	          <span className="rd-demo-label">Out loud</span>
219	          <div className="rd-voice-stack">
220	            <AnimatePresence mode="popLayout" initial={false}>
221	              {!gone && (phase === "tell" || phase === "colour") && (
222	                <motion.div
223	                  key={`tell-${sentence}`}
224	                  className="rd-bubble"
225	                  layout
226	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 14, scale: 0.98 }}
227	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0, scale: 1 }}
228	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, y: -10, transition: { duration: 0.25 } }}
229	                  transition={{ duration: 0.45, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
230	                >
231	                  <span className="rd-bubble-head">
232	                    <Mic size={12} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
233	                    You say
234	                    <Waveform live={phase === "tell"} />
235	                  </span>
236	                  <p>
237	                    “{tellText}
238	                    {phase === "tell" && <span className="rd-caret" aria-hidden="true" />}”
239	                  </p>
240	                </motion.div>
241	              )}
242	              {!gone && phase === "colour" && (
243	                <motion.div
244	                  key={`colour-${sentence}`}
245	                  className="rd-colour"
246	                  layout
247	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, scale: 0.9, rotate: -1.5 }}
248	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1, rotate: 0 }}
249	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, transition: { duration: 0.2 } }}
250	                  transition={{ type: "spring", stiffness: 380, damping: 26 }}
251	                >
252	                  <span className="rd-colour-head">Colour it</span>
253	                  <p>“{COLOUR[sentence]}”</p>
254	                </motion.div>
255	              )}
256	              {gone && (
257	                <motion.div
258	                  key="recall"
259	                  className="rd-bubble rd-bubble-recall"
260	                  layout
261	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 14 }}
262	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
263	                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
264	                  transition={{ duration: 0.45, delay: 0.35, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
265	                >
266	                  <span className="rd-bubble-head">
267	                    <Mic size={12} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
268	                    The Recall
269	                    <Waveform live={phase === "recall"} />
270	                  </span>
271	                  <p>
272	                    “{recallText}
273	                    {phase === "recall" && <span className="rd-caret" aria-hidden="true" />}”
274	                  </p>
275	                </motion.div>
276	              )}
277	              {showTally && (
278	                <motion.div
279	                  key="tally"
280	                  className="rd-tally"
281	                  layout
282	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 10 }}
283	                  animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
284	                  exit={{ opacity: 0 }}
285	                  transition={{ duration: 0.4, ease: [0.22, 1, 0.36, 1] }}
286	                >
287	                  <span className="rd-tally-head">
288	                    <strong>
289	                      {carried} of {DETAILS.length}
290	                    </strong>{" "}
291	                    details carried
292	                  </span>
293	                  <ul className="rd-tally-list">
294	                    {DETAILS.map((d, i) => (
295	                      <motion.li
296	                        key={d.label}
297	                        data-carried={d.carried || undefined}
298	                        initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0, y: 4 }}
299	                        animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
300	                        transition={{ delay: 0.15 + i * 0.12, duration: 0.3 }}
301	                      >
302	                        {d.label}
303	                      </motion.li>
304	                    ))}
305	                  </ul>
306	                </motion.div>
307	              )}
308	              {!gone && phase === "read" && (
309	                <motion.p
310	                  key={`quiet-${sentence}`}
311	                  className="rd-quiet"
312	                  initial={reduced ? false : { opacity: 0 }}
313	                  animate={{ opacity: 1 }}
314	                  exit={{ opacity: 0, transition: { duration: 0.15 } }}
315	                  transition={{ duration: 0.4 }}
316	                >
317	                  Reading silently. Nothing comes out yet.
318	                </motion.p>
319	              )}
320	            </AnimatePresence>
321	          </div>
322	        </div>
323	      </div>
324	      <p className="rd-demo-foot">Demonstration. The stimulus and the transcript are written for this page.</p>
325	    </div>
326	  );
327	}

1363	   padding, which is what a caption looks like, and the student it was built
1364	   for read the score off it and asked where the graded responses were
1365	   (Celia, 2026-09-08). Bordered, full width, and 44px of target. */
1366	.sl-sec-attempt {
1367	  align-items: center;
1368	  background: var(--rd-paper, #fff);
1369	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.12));
1370	  border-radius: 10px;
1371	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
1372	  cursor: pointer;
1373	  display: flex;
1374	  flex-wrap: wrap;
1375	  font: inherit;
1376	  font-size: 13.5px;
1377	  gap: 4px 12px;
1378	  justify-content: space-between;
1379	  min-height: 44px;
1380	  padding: 8px 14px;
1381	  text-align: left;
1382	  transition:
1383	    border-color 160ms ease,
1384	    color 160ms ease;
1385	  width: 100%;
1386	}
1387	.sl-sec-attempt[data-flat="true"] {
1388	  background: none;
1389	  border-style: dashed;
1390	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
1391	  cursor: default;
1392	}
1393	.sl-sec-attempt:hover:not([data-flat="true"]) {
1394	  border-color: currentColor;
1395	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1396	}
1397	/* What the row opens, said on the row rather than left to be guessed. */
1398	.sl-sec-attempt-go {
1399	  font-size: 12.5px;
1400	  font-weight: 600;
1401	  white-space: nowrap;
1402	}
1403	.sl-sec-attempt:not([data-flat="true"]) .sl-sec-attempt-go {
1404	  text-decoration: underline;
1405	  text-underline-offset: 3px;
1406	}
1407	.sl-sec-go {
1408	  appearance: none;
1409	  cursor: pointer;
1410	  display: inline-flex;
1411	  align-items: center;
1412	  min-height: 44px;
1413	  padding: 0 22px;
1414	  font: inherit;
1415	  font-size: 14px;
1416	  font-weight: 600;
1417	  color: var(--rd-cream);
1418	  background: var(--rd-ink);
1419	  border: 0;
1420	  border-radius: 9999px;
1421	  white-space: nowrap;
1422	  transition:
1423	    background 0.2s ease,
1424	    transform 0.35s var(--rd-ease);
1425	}
1426	.sl-sec-go:hover:not(:disabled) {
1427	  background: var(--rd-indigo-2);
1428	  transform: translateY(-1px);
1429	}
1430	.sl-sec-go:disabled {
1431	  opacity: 0.5;
1432	  cursor: default;
1433	}
1434	
1435	/* ---- the four numbers ---- */
1436	.sl-stats {
1437	  display: grid;
1438	  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
1439	  background: var(--rd-paper);
1440	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
1441	  border-radius: var(--rd-radius);
1442	  overflow: hidden;
1443	}
1444	.sl-stat {
1445	  padding: 20px 22px 22px;
1446	  border-left: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
1447	}
1448	.sl-stat:first-child {
1449	  border-left: 0;
1450	}
1451	.sl-sv {
1452	  display: block;
1453	  margin-top: 8px;
1454	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
1455	  font-weight: 400;
1456	  font-size: 34px;
1457	  line-height: 1;
1458	  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
1459	  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
1460	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1461	}
1462	.sl-sv small {
1463	  font-size: 16px;
1464	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
1465	}
1466	.sl-sv[data-dir="down"] {
1467	  color: var(--rd-emerald);
1468	}
1469	.sl-sv[data-empty] {
1470	  font-family: var(--rd-sans);
1471	  font-size: 15px;
1472	  font-weight: 500;
1473	  letter-spacing: 0;
1474	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
1475	}
1476	.sl-sd {
1477	  display: block;
1478	  margin-top: 6px;
1479	  font-size: 13px;
1480	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
1481	  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
1482	}
1483	
1484	/* ---- her runs ---- */
1485	.sl-scroll {
1486	  overflow-x: auto;
1487	  margin-top: 14px;
1488	}
1489	.sl-rtable {
1490	  width: 100%;
1491	  border-collapse: collapse;
1492	  font-size: 14px;
1493	}
1494	.sl-rtable th {
1495	  text-align: left;
1496	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
1497	  font-size: 10.5px;
1498	  letter-spacing: 0.14em;
1499	  text-transform: uppercase;
1500	  font-weight: 400;
1501	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
1502	  padding: 0 12px 10px 0;
1503	}
1504	.sl-rtable th.sl-r,
1505	.sl-rtable td.sl-r {
1506	  text-align: right;
1507	  padding-right: 0;
1508	}
1509	.sl-rtable td {
1510	  padding: 12px 12px 12px 0;
1511	  border-top: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
1512	  font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;
1513	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
1514	  white-space: nowrap;
1515	}
1516	.sl-rtable td:first-child {
1517	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1518	  font-weight: 500;
1519	}
1520	.sl-ticks {
1521	  display: inline-flex;
1522	  gap: 3px;
1523	  justify-content: flex-end;
1524	}
1525	.sl-tick {
1526	  width: 5px;
1527	  height: 16px;
1528	  border-radius: 2px;
1529	  background: var(--rd-emerald);
1530	}
1531	.sl-tick[data-state="notJudged"] {
1532	  background: var(--rd-paper);
1533	  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--rd-emerald);
1534	}
1535	.sl-tick[data-state="silent"] {
1536	  background: var(--rd-line);
1537	  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--rd-ink-3);
1538	}
1539	.sl-tick[data-state="missing"] {
1540	  background: var(--rd-line);
1541	}
1542	.sl-skel {
1543	  display: inline-block;
1544	  height: 15px;
1545	  border-radius: 4px;
1546	  background: var(--rd-line-2);
1547	  animation: sl-pulse 1.4s ease-in-out infinite;
1548	}
1549	@keyframes sl-pulse {
1550	  0%,
1551	  100% {
1552	    opacity: 1;
1553	  }
1554	  50% {
1555	    opacity: 0.45;
1556	  }
1557	}
1558	
1559	/* ============================================================== close */
1560	
1561	.rd-close {
1562	  padding: 104px 14px 14px;
1563	}
1564	.rd-close-panel {
1565	  position: relative;
1566	  overflow: hidden;
1567	  display: flex;
1568	  flex-direction: column;
1569	  align-items: center;
1570	  gap: 34px;
1571	  padding: clamp(72px, 12vh, 128px) 24px;
1572	  text-align: center;
1573	  color: var(--rd-on-dark);
1574	  border-radius: var(--rd-radius-panel);
1575	  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #1a1635 0%, #2a2450 38%, #5b4f8f 78%, #a89fe0 100%);
1576	}
1577	.rd-close-panel::after {
1578	  content: "";
1579	  position: absolute;
1580	  inset: auto -10% -35% -10%;
1581	  height: 60%;
1582	  border-radius: 50%;
1583	  background: radial-gradient(ellipse at center, rgba(243, 201, 160, 0.55), rgba(243, 201, 160, 0) 65%);
1584	  pointer-events: none;
1585	}
1586	.rd-close-line {
1587	  position: relative;
1588	  z-index: 1;
1589	  margin: 0;
1590	  max-width: 20ch;
1591	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
1592	  font-weight: 400;
1593	  font-variation-settings: "opsz" 144, "SOFT" 30;
1594	  font-size: clamp(32px, 4.4vw, 58px);
1595	  line-height: 1.04;
1596	  letter-spacing: -0.026em;
1597	  text-wrap: balance;
1598	}
1599	.rd-close-line em {
1600	  font-weight: 300;
1601	  color: var(--rd-apricot-light);
1602	}
1603	.rd-close .sl-start {
1604	  position: relative;
1605	  z-index: 1;
1606	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1607	  background: var(--rd-cream);
1608	  box-shadow: 0 18px 30px -18px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.6);
1609	}
1610	.rd-close .sl-start:hover {
1611	  background: var(--rd-on-dark);
1612	}
1613	.rd-close :focus-visible {
1614	  outline-color: var(--rd-on-dark);
1615	}
1616	
1617	/* ========================================================= responsive */
1618	
1619	@media (max-width: 1100px) {
1620	  .sl-beats {
1621	    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
1622	  }
1623	  .rd-counts-grid {
1624	    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
1625	  }
1626	  .rd-count-note {
1627	    padding-top: 14px;
1628	  }
1629	}
1630	
1631	@media (max-width: 900px) {
1632	  .rd-hero-panel {
1633	    min-height: 0;
1634	  }
1635	  .rd-hero-space {
1636	    min-height: 720px;
1637	  }
1638	  .mw-stage {
1639	    bottom: 128px;
1640	    padding: 0 14px;
1641	  }
1642	  .rd-demo-body {
1643	    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
1644	  }
1645	  .rd-paper {
1646	    border-right: 0;
1647	    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
1648	    min-height: 0;
1649	  }
1650	  .rd-voice {
1651	    min-height: 220px;
1652	  }
1653	  .rd-method,
1654	  .rd-counts,
1655	  .rd-slab {
1656	    padding-top: 72px;
1657	  }
1658	  .rd-close {
1659	    padding-top: 72px;
1660	  }
1661	  .sl-dr-view,
1662	  .sl-runs {
1663	    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
1664	  }
1665	  .sl-dr-side {
1666	    position: static;
1667	    flex-direction: row;
1668	    flex-wrap: wrap;
1669	  }
1670	  .sl-stats {
1671	    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
1672	  }
1673	  .sl-stat:nth-child(3) {
1674	    border-left: 0;
1675	  }
1676	  .sl-stat:nth-child(n + 3) {
1677	    border-top: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
1678	  }
1679	}
1680	
1681	@media (max-width: 640px) {
1682	  .rd-hero {
1683	    padding: 8px 8px 0;
1684	  }
1685	  .rd-hero-panel {
1686	    border-radius: 22px;
1687	  }
1688	  .rd-hero-top {
1689	    padding: 18px 18px 0;
1690	  }
1691	  .rd-meta {
1692	    display: none;
1693	  }
1694	  .rd-hero-copy {
1695	    padding: 28px 16px 0;
1696	  }
1697	  .sl-title {
1698	    font-size: clamp(40px, 12vw, 56px);
1699	  }
1700	  .sl-cta {
1701	    flex-direction: column;
1702	  }
1703	  .sl-start {
1704	    width: 100%;
1705	    justify-content: center;
1706	  }
1707	  .rd-hero-space {
1708	    min-height: 760px;
1709	  }
1710	  .rd-demo-title {
1711	    display: none;
1712	  }
1713	  .sl-beats {
1714	    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
1715	  }
1716	  .rd-method,
1717	  .rd-counts,
1718	  .rd-slab {
1719	    padding-left: 12px;
1720	    padding-right: 12px;
1721	  }
1722	  .sl-dr-tabs {
1723	    padding: 12px 20px 0;
1724	  }
1725	  .sl-dr-note {
1726	    display: none;
1727	  }
1728	  .sl-dr-view {
1729	    padding: 24px 20px 26px;
1730	  }
1731	  .sl-runs > div:first-child {
1732	    padding: 20px 20px 10px;
1733	  }
1734	  .sl-section {
1735	    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
1736	  }
1737	  .sl-sec-go {
1738	    justify-self: start;
1739	  }
1740	  .sl-stats {
1741	    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
1742	  }
1743	  .sl-stat {
1744	    border-left: 0;
1745	    border-top: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
1746	  }
1747	  .sl-stat:first-child {
1748	    border-top: 0;
1749	  }
1750	  .rd-close {
1751	    padding: 72px 8px 8px;
1752	  }
1753	  .rd-close-panel {
1754	    border-radius: 22px;
1755	  }
1756	}
1757	
1758	/* Motion is a courtesy, never the mechanism. Every state above is reachable
1759	   with this on; continuous loops stop rather than speed up. */
1760	@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
1761	  .sl *,
1762	  .sl *::before,
1763	  .sl *::after {
1764	    animation: none !important;
1765	    transition: none !important;
1766	  }
1767	  .mw-layer,
1768	  .mw-stage,
1769	  .rd-hero-copy {
1770	    will-change: auto;
1771	  }
1772	}
1773	
1774	/* ------------------------------------------------------- the briefing page
1775	   The instructions on their own page, before anything records. One column,
1776	   everything open, Start at the top and again at the foot. */
1777	.sl-briefing-page {
1778	  max-width: 880px;
1779	  margin: 0 auto;
1780	  padding: 40px 20px 96px;
1781	  display: grid;
1782	  gap: 32px;
1783	}
1784	/* Stacked on purpose. It was space-between, and at this page's width the
1785	   title block and the CTA never fit on one line, so the CTA always wrapped
1786	   under the tagline: the layout that shipped was accidental. */
1787	.sl-bp-head {
1788	  display: flex;
1789	  flex-direction: column;
1790	  gap: 22px;
1791	  align-items: flex-start;
1792	}
1793	/* The notice, un-boxed. It shared fill, hairline and radius with the four step
1794	   cards below it, so the one sentence written to lower her shoulders read as a
1795	   fifth thing to do. A plain line in ink, the page speaking. */
1796	.sl-briefing-page .sl-notice {
1797	  padding: 0;
1798	  background: none;
1799	  border: 0;
1800	  border-radius: 0;
1801	  font-size: 17px;
1802	  line-height: 1.5;
1803	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1804	  max-width: 62ch;
1805	}
1806	/* A landing-page pill on a task page. The lift and outer shadow are the
1807	   door's persuade register; on paper the fill darkening is enough. */
1808	.sl-briefing-page .sl-start,
1809	.sl-briefing-page .sl-start:hover,
1810	.sl-briefing-page .sl-start:active {
1811	  transform: none;
1812	  box-shadow: none;
1813	}
1814	/* The hero classes are written for the door's dark purple ground: .sl-title is
1815	   white at up to 96px, .sl-tagline is white with a drop shadow. This page sits
1816	   on paper, so both were being drawn in white on cream. The briefing page uses
1817	   the same classes for the same copy, so it restates the colour and the size
1818	   here rather than forking them. */
1819	/* The focus ring, per control rather than once at the top.
1820	   `.sl :focus-visible` sets a ring for the whole world, and loses the cascade on
1821	   the two button classes, so Start and Back matched :focus-visible and computed
1822	   outline-style: none. A keyboard student could not see where she was on the one
1823	   control that begins the run, and Back had no indicator at all.
1824	
1825	   The colour depends on the ground, and these buttons appear on both: the door
1826	   draws them on the dark hero panel, every other surface on cream. */
1827	.sl .sl-start:focus-visible,
1828	.sl .sl-brief:focus-visible {
1829	  outline: 2px solid var(--rd-ink);
1830	  outline-offset: 3px;
1831	}
1832	.rd-hero .sl-start:focus-visible,
1833	.rd-hero .sl-brief:focus-visible {
1834	  outline-color: var(--rd-cream);
1835	}
1836	/* 43x44 was under the 44x44 floor, and this is the exit control. */
1837	.sl-brief {
1838	  min-width: 44px;
1839	}
1840	
1841	/* Two more hero classes drawn in white for the door's dark panel. On paper they
1842	   were invisible: the drill's own name, and a Back button nobody could see. */
1843	.sl-briefing-page .sl-chip {
1844	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1845	  background: var(--rd-paper);
1846	  border-color: var(--rd-line);
1847	  backdrop-filter: none;
1848	}
1849	.sl-briefing-page .sl-brief {
1850	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
1851	  text-decoration-color: var(--rd-line);
1852	}
1853	.sl-briefing-page .sl-brief:hover {
1854	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1855	  text-decoration-color: var(--rd-ink-2);
1856	}
1857	/* A heading inside the long version was set larger than the section heading
1858	   above it, so the page read as if the essay outranked its own label. */
1859	.sl-briefing-page .sl-bp-essay h3 {
1860	  font-size: 20px;
1861	  margin-top: 28px;
1862	}
1863	.sl-briefing-page .sl-title {
1864	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1865	  font-size: clamp(30px, 3.4vw, 46px);
1866	  line-height: 1.06;
1867	  letter-spacing: -0.02em;
1868	  margin: 14px 0 0;
1869	}
1870	.sl-briefing-page .sl-title em {
1871	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
1872	}
1873	/* .sl-tagline is white with a drop shadow, which is right on the door's dark
1874	   hero and unreadable here: this page sits on paper. The briefing page reuses
1875	   the class for the same line of copy, so it restates the colour rather than
1876	   forking the class. */
1877	.sl-briefing-page .sl-tagline {
1878	  margin: 12px 0 0;
1879	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
1880	  text-shadow: none;
1881	}
1882	.sl-briefing-page .sl-tagline strong {
1883	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1884	}
1885	/* The long version, closed by default. */
1886	.sl-bp-more > summary {
1887	  cursor: pointer;
1888	  list-style: none;
1889	  padding: 14px 0;
1890	  border-top: 1px solid var(--rd-line);
1891	}
1892	/* The summary's heading used to inherit body weight, so the only help on the
1893	   page looked like a stray sentence beside an uppercase h2. Same treatment as
1894	   its sibling, and a drawn chevron instead of a " +" that some readers spoke. */
1895	.sl-bp-more > summary {
1896	  display: flex;
1897	  align-items: center;
1898	  gap: 10px;
1899	}
1900	.sl-bp-more > summary > h2 {
1901	  display: inline;
1902	  margin: 0;
1903	}
1904	.sl-bp-more > summary::-webkit-details-marker {
1905	  display: none;
1906	}
1907	.sl-bp-more > summary::after {
1908	  content: "";
1909	  width: 7px;
1910	  height: 7px;
1911	  border-right: 1.5px solid var(--rd-ink-2);
1912	  border-bottom: 1.5px solid var(--rd-ink-2);
1913	  transform: translateY(-2px) rotate(45deg);
1914	  transition: transform 180ms var(--rd-ease);
1915	}
1916	.sl-bp-more[open] > summary::after {
1917	  transform: translateY(2px) rotate(225deg);
1918	}
1919	.sl-bp-more > summary:focus-visible {
1920	  outline: 2px solid var(--rd-ink);
1921	  outline-offset: 3px;
1922	}
1923	/* --rd-ink-soft is not a token this file defines, so both of these were falling
1924	   through to the global muted colour and landing at 5.9:1 on cream: fainter than
1925	   the body copy they sit beside. They use the real tokens now. */
1926	/* One micro-label style, the mono one the file declares as such. Section
1927	   headings and step numbers were two different Inter uppercase styles beside
1928	   the mono chip: three looks for one rank. */
1929	.sl-bp-h2 {
1930	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
1931	  font-size: 12px;
1932	  font-weight: 500;
1933	  text-transform: uppercase;
1934	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
1935	  margin-bottom: 16px;
1936	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1937	}
1938	.sl-bp-list {
1939	  display: grid;
1940	  gap: 14px;
1941	  list-style: none;
1942	  padding: 0;
1943	  margin: 0;
1944	}
1945	.sl-bp-list li {
1946	  border: 1px solid var(--rd-line, hsl(var(--border)));
1947	  border-radius: 16px;
1948	  padding: 18px 20px;
1949	}
1950	.sl-bp-n {
1951	  display: block;
1952	  font-family: var(--rd-mono);
1953	  font-size: 11px;
1954	  letter-spacing: 0.16em;
1955	  text-transform: uppercase;
1956	  color: var(--rd-ink-3);
1957	  margin-bottom: 6px;
1958	}
1959	.sl-bp-list li strong {
1960	  display: block;
1961	  font-size: 18px;
1962	  margin-bottom: 6px;
1963	}
1964	/* The sentences she has to follow ran past 100 characters a line. The essay
1965	   already holds 68ch; the steps get a measure too. */
1966	.sl-bp-list li p {
1967	  margin: 0;
1968	  line-height: 1.6;
1969	  max-width: 62ch;
1970	}
1971	.sl-bp-foot p {
1972	  margin: 0;
1973	  max-width: 62ch;
1974	  color: var(--rd-ink-2);
1975	}
1976	.sl-bp-fact {
1977	  font-size: 17px;
1978	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1979	}
1980	.sl-bp-foot .sl-bp-fact {
1981	  color: var(--rd-ink);
1982	}
1983	.sl-bp-closer {
1984	  font-family: var(--rd-display);
1985	  font-style: italic;
1986	  font-size: 19px;
1987	}
1988	.sl-bp-foot {

1997	@keyframes sl-bp-rise {

2041	/* ---- the briefing page's type scale ----
2042	   Sizes on this page had drifted to 16, 16.5, 17, 18, 19 and 20px: six steps
2043	   where the eye can tell apart three. One scale, in rem so it follows her

2045	     label 0.6875  body 1  lead 1.1875  step 1.375  title clamp(2, 3.4vw, 3)
2046	   Roles: mono for labels, Inter for anything she reads at length, Fraunces

2048	.sl-briefing-page {

1	import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
2	import { listMySectionGrades } from "../api/activitiesClient";
3	import type { SectionGradeStub } from "../api/types";
4	
5	/**
6	 * Every graded Read, Recall, Colour run this student has, oldest to newest.
7	 *
8	 * Each finished section is already stored whole -- its coverage, its per-line
9	 * marking, its transcript and the recording behind it -- but until now the only
10	 * way to see one was to remember which Attempt it was and open it. A student
11	 * who runs a section a week has no way of knowing whether the weeks are adding
12	 * up to anything, and that is the entire point of grading her.
13	 *
14	 * So: one row per graded run, in date order, with the number that moves. The
15	 * bars are the same number drawn, because a column of percentages is a list and
16	 * a shape is a trend.
17	 */
18	export function SectionProgress({
19	  loadGrades = listMySectionGrades,
20	  onOpen,
21	}: {
22	  loadGrades?: () => Promise<SectionGradeStub[]>;
23	  onOpen?: (assignmentId: string) => void;
24	}) {
25	  const [runs, setRuns] = useState<SectionGradeStub[] | null>(null);
26	  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
27	
28	  useEffect(() => {
29	    let cancelled = false;
30	    void (async () => {
31	      try {
32	        const rows = await loadGrades();
33	        if (!cancelled) setRuns(rows);
34	      } catch {
35	        if (!cancelled) setFailed(true);
36	      }
37	    })();
38	    return () => {
39	      cancelled = true;
40	    };
41	  }, [loadGrades]);
42	
43	  if (failed) {
44	    return (
45	      <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-6">
46	        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide text-foreground">Your progress</h2>
47	        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
48	          Your past runs could not be loaded just now. They are saved — try again in a moment.
49	        </p>
50	      </section>
51	    );
52	  }
53	
54	  if (runs === null) {
55	    return (
56	      <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-6">
57	        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide text-foreground">Your progress</h2>
58	        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">Loading your past runs…</p>
59	      </section>
60	    );
61	  }
62	
63	  if (runs.length === 0) {
64	    return (
65	      <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-6">
66	        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide text-foreground">Your progress</h2>
67	        <p className="mt-2 max-w-[60ch] text-sm text-muted-foreground">
68	          No graded runs yet. Finish a section below and its marked report is saved here — every
69	          run you do from then on shows up in this list.
70	        </p>
71	      </section>
72	    );
73	  }
74	
75	  // Oldest first: the eye reads left to right, and improvement should run that
76	  // way too. The query hands them back newest first.
77	  const ordered = [...runs].reverse();
78	  const latest = ordered[ordered.length - 1];
79	  const first = ordered[0];
80	  const change = Math.round(pct(latest) - pct(first));
81	
82	  return (
83	    <section className="rounded-2xl border border-border/60 bg-card/40 p-6">
84	      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline justify-between gap-3">
85	        <h2 className="text-sm font-semibold tracking-wide text-foreground">Your progress</h2>
86	        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
87	          {ordered.length} graded {ordered.length === 1 ? "run" : "runs"}
88	          {ordered.length > 1 && (
89	            <>
90	              {" · "}
91	              {change > 0 ? `up ${change}` : change < 0 ? `down ${Math.abs(change)}` : "level"}
92	              {change === 0 ? "" : " points"} since your first
93	            </>
94	          )}
95	        </p>
96	      </div>
97	
98	      <div className="mt-5 flex h-24 items-end gap-1.5" aria-hidden="true">
99	        {ordered.map((r) => (
100	          <div
101	            key={`${r.assignment_id}-${r.attempt_no}`}
102	            className="min-w-[6px] flex-1 rounded-t bg-primary/70"
103	            style={{ height: `${Math.max(4, pct(r))}%` }}
104	          />
105	        ))}
106	      </div>
107	
108	      <ul className="mt-5 divide-y divide-border/50">
109	        {[...ordered].reverse().map((r) => (
110	          <li key={`${r.assignment_id}-${r.attempt_no}`}>
111	            <button
112	              type="button"
113	              onClick={() => onOpen?.(r.assignment_id)}
114	              disabled={!onOpen}
115	              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between gap-4 py-3 text-left disabled:cursor-default"
116	            >
117	              <span className="min-w-0">
118	                <span className="block truncate text-sm text-foreground">
119	                  {r.source_section || "Section"}
120	                  {r.attempt_no > 1 ? ` · attempt ${r.attempt_no}` : ""}
121	                </span>
122	                <span className="block text-xs text-muted-foreground">
123	                  {formatWhen(r.graded_at)}
124	                  {r.section?.total_seconds
125	                    ? ` · ${Math.round(r.section.total_seconds / 60)} min`
126	                    : ""}
127	                  {r.section?.reciting_count ? ` · ${r.section.reciting_count} recited` : ""}
128	                </span>
129	              </span>
130	              <span className="shrink-0 text-sm font-semibold tabular-nums text-foreground">
131	                {Math.round(pct(r))}%
132	              </span>
133	            </button>
134	          </li>
135	        ))}
136	      </ul>
137	    </section>
138	  );
139	}
140	
141	/** Coverage is stored 0-1 in some runs and 0-100 in others; read both. */
142	function pct(r: SectionGradeStub): number {
143	  const v = r.section?.mean_coverage ?? 0;
144	  const n = v <= 1 ? v * 100 : v;
145	  return Math.max(0, Math.min(100, n));
146	}
147	
148	function formatWhen(iso: string): string {
149	  const d = new Date(iso);
150	  if (Number.isNaN(d.getTime())) return "";
151	  return d.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: "short", day: "numeric", year: "numeric" });
152	}

1	/**
2	 * A preview harness for the section launch surface. Development only.
3	 *
4	 * It exists so the component can be looked at in a real browser, in every state
5	 * and at every breakpoint, without signing in and without a graded section
6	 * sitting in the database. Only the network is stubbed: `window.fetch` is
7	 * replaced before anything imports the Supabase client, so the component, the
8	 * client helper and the derivation all run exactly as they do in the product.
9	 *
10	 * Not routed and not imported by the app. Reached at

12	 * point in the Vite build, which only builds `index.html`, so it never ships.
13	 *
14	 * States: ?state=runs|oneRun|empty|failed|noHistory|library  ·  ?theme=light|dark
15	 */
16	import { createRoot } from "react-dom/client";
17	// The section JSONs deliberately ship an empty instructions block -- the rules

22	
23	const params = new URLSearchParams(location.search);

26	
27	function stim(item_order: number, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
28	  return {
29	    slug: `s${item_order}`,
30	    item_order,
31	    carried: 3,
32	    total: 3,
33	    percent: 100,
34	    band: "at-the-standard",
35	    missed: [],
36	    decoysCarried: [],
37	    unjudged: [],
38	    reciting: false,
39	    listing: false,
40	    coloured: true,

42	    dead_air_seconds: 0,
43	    spoken: "she said something here",
44	    ...over,
45	  };
46	}

48	const SLOTS = 25;
49	function run(seconds: number, createdAt: string, gaps: number[] = [], silent: number[] = []) {
50	  return {
51	    assignment_id: `assign-${seconds}`,
52	    attempt_no: 1,
53	    source_section: "PT105.S2",
54	    graded_at: createdAt,
55	    created_at: createdAt,
56	    stimuli: Array.from({ length: SLOTS }, (_, i) => {
57	      const n = i + 1;
58	      if (silent.includes(n)) return stim(n, { spoken: "", carried: 0, percent: 0 });
59	      if (gaps.includes(n)) return stim(n, { ungraded: "judge returned nothing" });
60	      return stim(n);
61	    }),
62	    section: {
63	      accommodation: "standard",
64	      goal_minutes: 25,
65	      total_seconds: seconds,
66	      within_time: seconds <= 1500,
67	      within_goal: seconds <= 1500,
68	      mean_coverage: 91,
69	      stimuli_graded: SLOTS,
70	      stimuli_in_section: SLOTS,

72	      listing_count: 0,
73	      uncoloured_count: 0,
74	      worst_dead_air_seconds: 4,
75	      dead_air_threshold_seconds: 8,
76	      decoy_failures: 0,

79	  };

1	import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
2	import { ArrowRight } from "lucide-react";
3	import type { ActivityInstructions } from "../api/types";
4	import {
5	  readMyRecentSectionGrades,
6	  type RecentSectionRun,
7	} from "../api/activitiesClient";
8	import { SectionLaunchFrame } from "./SectionLaunchFrame";
9	import { clock, signedClock, statsFor, ticksFor } from "../recall/runStats";
10	import "./section-launch.css";
11	import { useNoKeyboard } from "../recall/noKeyboard";
12	
13	/** How many past runs the history block shows. */
14	const RUNS_SHOWN = 3;
15	
16	export interface SectionLaunchProps {
17	  title: string;
18	  subtitle: string | null;
19	  instructions: ActivityInstructions;
20	  /** Stimuli in this section, from the assignment snapshot. */
21	  itemCount?: number;
22	  /** The clock this section is run against. */
23	  timeGoalMinutes?: number | null;
24	  /** `PT105.S2`. Scopes the history read to this section. */
25	  sourceSection?: string | null;
26	  /**
27	   * Whose runs to read. Passed to the QUERY, not used as a display filter: the
28	   * admin SELECT policy on `activity_section_grades` returns every student's
29	   * rows, and no flag on this component can undo that. Only the `.eq` can.
30	   */
31	  studentId?: string | null;
32	  /**
33	   * Whether to show run history at all. False for a teacher reading a student's
34	   * page and for the authoring preview, where "her last three runs" is either
35	   * somebody else's record or nobody's.
36	   */
37	  showHistory?: boolean;
38	  onStart: () => void;
39	  onExit?: () => void;
40	}
41	
42	type HistoryState =
43	  | { kind: "idle" }
44	  | { kind: "loading" }
45	  | { kind: "ready"; runs: RecentSectionRun[] }
46	  | { kind: "failed" };
47	
48	/**
49	 * The door to a Read, Recall, Colour section.
50	 *
51	 * What it has to do that the editorial intro could not: fit. The page it
52	 * replaces ran 2778px, put its one button below the fold and grew a sticky dock
53	 * to compensate. This is one card, Start in the top right, and the long briefing
54	 * folded into a drawer that opens a beat at a time.
55	 *
56	 * Everything numeric on it is derived from runs she has already done -- see
57	 * `recall/runStats.ts`, which holds the arithmetic and the refusals. Nothing
58	 * here is scored, predicted, or filled in when the evidence is missing.
59	 */
60	export function SectionLaunch({
61	  title,
62	  subtitle,
63	  instructions,
64	  itemCount,
65	  timeGoalMinutes,
66	  sourceSection,
67	  studentId,
68	  showHistory = false,
69	  onStart,
70	  onExit,
71	}: SectionLaunchProps) {
72	  const [history, setHistory] = useState<HistoryState>({ kind: "idle" });
73	  const noKeyboard = useNoKeyboard();
74	
75	  const canRead = showHistory && Boolean(sourceSection) && Boolean(studentId);
76	
77	  useEffect(() => {
78	    if (!canRead) {
79	      setHistory({ kind: "idle" });
80	      return;
81	    }
82	    // A resolution that arrives after this effect has been superseded, or after
83	    // the component is gone, must not land. Without the guard a slow first read
84	    // can overwrite a fast second one and show the wrong section's runs.
85	    let live = true;
86	    setHistory({ kind: "loading" });
87	    readMyRecentSectionGrades(RUNS_SHOWN, {
88	      sourceSection: sourceSection ?? undefined,
89	      studentId: studentId ?? undefined,
90	    })
91	      .then((result) => {
92	        if (!live) return;
93	        setHistory(result.failed ? { kind: "failed" } : { kind: "ready", runs: result.runs });
94	      })
95	      .catch(() => {
96	        if (live) setHistory({ kind: "failed" });
97	      });
98	    return () => {
99	      live = false;
100	    };
101	  }, [canRead, sourceSection, studentId]);
102	
103	  // Derived inside the memo rather than above it. A `[]` literal computed on
104	  // every render is a new array every render, so hoisting it would give the
105	  // memo a dependency that never compares equal and recompute the stats each
106	  // pass -- a memo that memoises nothing.
107	  const runs = useMemo(
108	    () => (history.kind === "ready" ? history.runs : []),
109	    [history],
110	  );
111	  const stats = useMemo(
112	    () => statsFor(runs, timeGoalMinutes ?? null),
113	    [runs, timeGoalMinutes],
114	  );
115	
116	  return (
117	    <SectionLaunchFrame
118	      title={title}
119	      subtitle={subtitle}
120	      instructions={instructions}
121	      onExit={onExit}
122	      meta={
123	        <>
124	          {sourceSection ? `${sourceSection} · ` : ""}
125	          {typeof itemCount === "number" ? `${itemCount} stimuli` : ""}
126	          {timeGoalMinutes ? ` · est ${timeGoalMinutes} min` : ""}
127	        </>
128	      }
129	      primaryAction={
130	        <button type="button" className="sl-start" onClick={onStart}>
131	          Start the section
132	          <ArrowRight size={16} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
133	        </button>
134	      }
135	      closingAction={
136	        <button type="button" className="sl-start" onClick={onStart}>
137	          Start now
138	          <ArrowRight size={16} strokeWidth={2.4} aria-hidden="true" />
139	        </button>
140	      }
141	      /* Warned, not refused: Joshua's call, 2026-09-04. She is told what the
142	         run needs and left free to make the call herself, which is why nothing
143	         here disables Start. The run itself carries an on-screen way to advance
144	         so that going ahead is a real choice rather than a trap. */
145	      notice={
146	        noKeyboard ? (
147	          <p className="sl-notice" role="status">
148	            This one is built for a laptop. It records you for the whole section
149	            in one take, so a call or a locked screen ends the run, and the
150	            microphone on a phone hears you less well. You can carry on if you
151	            want to.
152	          </p>
153	        ) : null
154	      }
155	      strip={showHistory ? <StatStrip state={history} stats={stats} /> : null}
156	    >
157	      {showHistory && <RunHistory state={history} />}
158	    </SectionLaunchFrame>
159	  );
160	}
161	
162	/**
163	 * The four numbers, or an honest account of why there are not four numbers.
164	 *
165	 * A failed read and a student who has never run this section produce the same
166	 * empty array and must not produce the same sentence: one of them is being told
167	 * her work is gone.
168	 */
169	function StatStrip({
170	  state,
171	  stats,
172	}: {
173	  state: HistoryState;
174	  stats: ReturnType<typeof statsFor>;
175	}) {
176	  if (state.kind === "loading") {
177	    return (
178	      <div className="sl-stats" aria-hidden="true">
179	        {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
180	          <div className="sl-stat" key={i}>
181	            <span className="sl-label">&nbsp;</span>
182	            <span className="sl-sv">
183	              <span className="sl-skel" style={{ width: "60%" }} />
184	            </span>
185	          </div>
186	        ))}
187	      </div>
188	    );
189	  }
190	  if (state.kind === "failed" || state.kind === "idle") return null;
191	  if (stats.totalRuns === 0) return null;
192	
193	  return (
194	    <div className="sl-stats">
195	      <div className="sl-stat">
196	        <span className="sl-label">Last run</span>
197	        <span className="sl-sv" data-empty={stats.lastRunSeconds === null || undefined}>
198	          {stats.lastRunSeconds === null ? "no runs yet" : clock(stats.lastRunSeconds)}
199	        </span>
200	        <span className="sl-sd">{stats.totalRuns === 1 ? "first run" : "most recent"}</span>

{
  "task": "Work through the stimulus out loud, a piece at a time. When you have got all of it, press space. The text goes away, and you say the entire argument back from memory.",
  "coach": {
    "drill": "a whole Logical Reasoning section run out loud, where the student reads each line of a stimulus, looks away, tells it back from memory in her own words, reacts to it, and gives The Recall of the whole stimulus before moving to the next one",
    "rubric": [
      "Judge by meaning, never by wording. Her own words are required here, so a good recall will never match the line's phrasing. High overlap with the original is the failure, not the standard.",
      "Order is free. She is encouraged to deliver the ideas out of the order she read them in, and reordering must never cost her anything.",
      "Casual, unpolished, laughing delivery is expected. Never mark down for style, length or fluency.",
      "A detail merely gestured at is not carried. Naming the topic is not the same as saying what was claimed about it.",
      "Numbers, names, dates, quantities and connecting words are each their own detail. Never treat one as carried because the sentence around it was.",
      "A reaction that could be pasted onto any other sentence does not count as a colour. Judge whether it engages this content, never whether it is correct or well put.",
      "Score The Recall against the captured key for that stimulus, not against a retelling. A different name for the same move is fine; missing the move is not.",
      "Silence during a silent read is correct and expected. Only silence inside a recall or a colour counts against her."
    ],
    "partial_rule": "Mark 'partial' when the retelling is real but at least one detail went missing, which is the drill's ordinary state. Mark 'incorrect' only when the response is a gist or a topic label rather than a retelling."
  },
  "rules": [
    "Everything here is out loud. The whole section records in one take, and it never pauses.",
    "Read silently, about a sentence at a time. When you are not sure how much to take on, take less.",
    "Look away and tell it back in your own words. Then react to it. Both out loud.",
    "Keep going like that through the whole stimulus.",
    "Press space when you have got all of it. The stimulus vanishes and stays gone. Now say the whole argument back from memory, with every detail you can hold.",
    "Press space again and the next one loads. No breaks, no going back, all the way to the end."
  ],
  "tagline": "Read it, tell it back, react to it. Then the stimulus disappears and you say the whole argument back from memory.",
  "briefing": {
    "lede": "Worth reading properly once. After that, skim the rules above and go.",
    "closer": "One a day holds you steady. The second one is where you improve.",
    "sections": [
      {
        "body": [
          "You follow a stimulus, sentence by sentence, and it all makes sense as it goes. Then you get down to answer choice C and realise you are holding almost none of it. That is not a comprehension problem. It is a retention problem, and it is the one this drill was built for.",
          "Understanding is necessary for everything and sufficient for nothing. If the argument is not still with you while you work the answers, your understanding never got the chance to help.",
          "Most of what you call reading is built for getting to the end. Scanning names. Dropping the connecting words. Turning a list of three into one label. That is a fine way to read a phone bill. It is a bad way to read something that is about to ask you for every detail back."
        ],
        "heading": "You can understand every sentence and still arrive with nothing."
      },
      {
        "body": [
          "Three steps through the stimulus, then a fourth step once you have read all of it. You decide how much to take on at a time, and nothing on the screen is tracking where you are.",
          "Space is the only key, and you press it twice per stimulus. The first press takes the text away and asks you for the whole argument from memory. The second press moves you to the next stimulus. Nothing advances on its own, so a pause to think costs you nothing but the clock."
        ],
        "steps": [
          {
            "body": "Read about one sentence at a time, silently. Give each word enough attention to hear it naturally in your head. If that feels like too much to hold, take a shorter piece. When you take on too much, the part that slips is always the beginning.",
            "label": "Read it."
          },
          {
            "body": "Look away from the screen and tell it back in your own words, out loud, like you are telling a friend what it said. Out of order is fine. Casual is fine. Laughing is fine.",
            "label": "Tell it back."
          },
          {
            "body": "React. A doubt, a question, an example, a joke, a feeling. One or two seconds is plenty. It does not have to be clever, it has to be yours.",
            "label": "Colour it."
          },
          {
            "body": "Press space. The stimulus disappears and does not come back. Now say the entire argument back from memory, out loud, with every detail you can still hold. This is the part that gets scored.",
            "label": "The Recall."
          }
        ],
        "heading": "What you actually do."
      },
      {
        "body": [
          "Do not give back the gist, or the summary, or the important parts. You cannot know which parts were important until you have read the whole thing, so filtering on the way through mostly means quietly throwing out the piece you were going to need.",
          "Fewer words is fine when the words cover the same ideas. Fewer ideas is not. The names, the numbers, and the small words that carry the load (all, most, only, probably) either come back or they do not.",
          "**Efficiency is never a licence to leave information out.**"
        ],
        "heading": "Simpler is not the same as shorter."
      },
      {
        "body": [
          "You will open your mouth to colour something and find nothing there. That lasts about a week.",
          "It is not that you have no thoughts. It is that nobody has ever asked you for one in the middle of reading before, and the habit takes a few sessions to wake up. Say something obvious on purpose if you have to. \"Okay, sure.\" \"That seems like a stretch.\" \"Who measured that?\" All of those count.",
          "The reaction is what makes the sentence yours instead of the author's. Skip it and you are back to repeating words you do not own."
        ],
        "heading": "You will think you have nothing to say. Everybody does."
      },
      {
        "body": [
          "While you are working the stimulus, nothing on the screen hides. The whole thing stays readable, which means the only thing stopping you rereading a line while you tell it back is you, looking away from it.",
          "That is deliberate. Reading while you recall is a one way street with cars coming both ways. It jams. You stop using your memory at all and slide straight back into the author's exact words instead of finding your own.",
          "It is also the version that still works on paper, and on test day. A screen that hides the text for you is a crutch that cannot come with you."
        ],
        "heading": "Look away. That part is on you."
      },
      {
        "body": [
          "The goal is twenty-five minutes for a whole section, and it only counts if every stimulus got recalled, coloured, and given The Recall. Finishing early with four of them skipped is not finishing early. If you have extra time on test day, your goal scales with it.",
          "The clock in the corner shows how long you have been going. It is there so you can pace a section. It never counts down, because a number falling away in the corner of your eye produces exactly the skating read this drill exists to fix.",
          "It is not a race. It is the pace at which this stops being a study exercise and starts being how you read under a clock. Go slower while it is new. The speed shows up on its own once the method does."
        ],
        "heading": "Twenty-five minutes, and what that number means."
      }
    ]
  }
}

== useNoKeyboard
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

/**
 * Whether this device probably has no keyboard, so the drill's one key is out
 * of reach.
 *
 * A section advances on Space and on nothing else
 * (`SectionRunner.tsx`, "One key, and only Space"). That is deliberate: no
 * mouse in the loop, so her hands never leave the argument. It also means a
 * phone can open the drill, read stimulus 1, and never get to stimulus 2 --
 * the briefing, the door and the library all render fine at 390x844, so
 * nothing warns her before the recording is already running.
 *
 * `(pointer: coarse)` is the test rather than a width, because the question is
 * "is there a keyboard", not "is the screen small". A narrow desktop window has
 * a keyboard and must keep the pure one-key loop; a large tablet has no
 * keyboard and needs the control. Width answers neither.
 *
 * This is a hint, never a gate. Joshua's call, 2026-09-04: a phone is warned
 * and let through, not refused. So nothing here disables anything -- it adds a
 * sentence at the door and an on-screen way to advance inside the run, and
 * Space stays authoritative everywhere.
 */
export const NO_KEYBOARD_QUERY = "(pointer: coarse)";

/** Read once, outside React, so a non-browser render has a defined answer. */
export function hasNoKeyboard(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return false;
  return window.matchMedia(NO_KEYBOARD_QUERY).matches;
}

/**
 * The same question as a hook, kept live.
 *
 * A tablet with a keyboard case attached or removed mid-run changes the answer,
 * and the control appearing or going away is the correct response to that. The
 * initial state is computed rather than defaulted to `false`, so the first
 * paint on a phone already carries the control instead of flashing a screen
 * that cannot be advanced.
 */
export function useNoKeyboard(): boolean {
  const [noKeyboard, setNoKeyboard] = useState<boolean>(hasNoKeyboard);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (typeof window === "undefined" || typeof window.matchMedia !== "function") return;
    const mql = window.matchMedia(NO_KEYBOARD_QUERY);
    const onChange = () => setNoKeyboard(mql.matches);
    onChange();
    // `addEventListener` is not universal on MediaQueryList in older Safari,
    // which is exactly the population this query is about, so the older API is
    // the fallback rather than the assumption.
    if (typeof mql.addEventListener === "function") {
      mql.addEventListener("change", onChange);
      return () => mql.removeEventListener("change", onChange);
    }
    mql.addListener?.(onChange);
    return () => mql.removeListener?.(onChange);
  }, []);

  return noKeyboard;
